          "text": "## 2.1. Binder removal and leaching\nBinder removal experiments were carried out in batch mode using the 250 mL round-bottom flask with a temperature controlling facility. The flask was equipped with a magnetic stirrer for homogenization. Experiments were performed by using CJ with cathode and anode film. Optimum w/v ratio of film to CJ was found to be 50,000 mg/L at 90 ^∘C and 20 min for binder removal. Cu (anode) and Al (cathode) film were separate, washed with water and check its purity using XRD and XRF techniques. After the removal of Cu and Al the remaining solution was refluxed for 30 min and then filter to remove carbon and other impurities. The filtrate was used to check the leaching rate, using Eq. (2.1). Leaching rate is the percentage ratio of the amount of metal species in the leachate to the total amount, as measured by tri-acid digestion process (Uppsala, 2015) using ICP-MS.\nAmount of metal in the leachate Leaching rate 100 Total amount of metal found in tri acid digestion\nð2:1Þ",
